theory CAOT_ECC_v2_Routine begin

// Function signature and definition of the equational theory E

builtins: diffie-hellman, natural-numbers
functions: chain/2, fst/1, h/1, hkdf/3, kdfm/1, pair/2, pk/1, sdec/2,
           senc/2, sign/2, snd/1, true/0, verify/3
equations:
    fst(<x.1, x.2>) = x.1,
    sdec(senc(x.1, x.2), x.2) = x.1,
    snd(<x.1, x.2>) = x.2,
    verify(sign(x.1, x.2), x.1, pk(x.2)) = true

tactic: states_last
presort: s
prio: {id}
  regex"Fr\("
  regex"!LtkD\("
  regex"!KU\(~"
  regex"!KU\(sign"
deprio: {id}
  regex"Sess\("















restriction Equality:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  // safety formula

restriction Unique:
  "∀ x #i #j. ((Uniq( x ) @ #i) ∧ (Uniq( x ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  // safety formula

restriction OneKeyG:
  "∀ x #i #j. ((KeyG( x ) @ #i) ∧ (KeyG( x ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  // safety formula

restriction OneKeyD:
  "∀ x #i #j. ((KeyD( x ) @ #i) ∧ (KeyD( x ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  // safety formula

rule (modulo E) Gateway_Setup:
   [ Fr( ~skG ) ]
  --[ KeyG( $G ) ]->
   [ !LtkG( $G, ~skG ), !PkG( $G, 'g'^~skG ), Out( 'g'^~skG ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Device_Setup:
   [ Fr( ~skD ) ]
  --[ KeyD( $D ) ]->
   [ !LtkD( $D, ~skD ), !PkD( $D, pk(~skD) ), Out( pk(~skD) ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Reveal_Gateway:
   [ !LtkG( G, sk ) ] --[ RevG( G ) ]-> [ Out( sk ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Reveal_Device:
   [ !LtkD( D, sk ) ] --[ RevD( D ) ]-> [ Out( sk ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) R_Init_Send:
   [
   !LtkD( $D, skD ), !PkG( $G, pkG ), Fr( ~e ), Fr( ~n ), Fr( ~sid ),
   Fr( ~t )
   ]
  --[
  RInitSent( $D, $G, ~sid ),
  DevInit( $D, $G, ~sid,
           hkdf(pkG^~e, ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
  )
  ]->
   [
   DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid,
            hkdf(pkG^~e, ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>), %1,
            h(<'R0', 'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)

   Out( <<'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>, 
         sign(<'I', 'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>, skD)>
   )
   ]

  /*

rule (modulo E) R_Data_Send:
   [ DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0, %i, rh ), Fr( ~m ), Fr( ~t ) ]
  --[
  RSent( $D, $G, ~sid, ~m, %i ), RSentK( ck0, %i ),
  DevStep( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0 ), DevIdx( ck0, %i )
  ]->
   [
   DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0, (%i%+%1),
            h(<rh, <'D', $D, ~sid, %i, ~t>, 
               h(senc(<~m, 'D', $D, ~sid, %i, ~t>, kdfm(chain(ck0, %i))))>)
   ),
   Out( <<'D', $D, ~sid, %i, ~t>, 
         senc(<~m, 'D', $D, ~sid, %i, ~t>, kdfm(chain(ck0, %i)))>
   )
   ]

  // loop breaker: [0]
  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) R_Data_Recv:
   [
   SrvSess( $D, $G, sid, ck0, %i, rh ),
   In( <<'D', $D, sid, %i, t>, 
        senc(<m, 'D', $D, sid, %i, t>, kdfm(chain(ck0, %i)))>
   )
   ]
  --[
  RRecv( $D, $G, sid, m, %i ), RRecvK( ck0, %i ),
  SrvStep( $D, $G, sid, ck0 ), SrvIdx( ck0, %i )
  ]->
   [
   SrvSess( $D, $G, sid, ck0, (%i%+%1),
            h(<rh, <'D', $D, sid, %i, t>, 
               h(senc(<m, 'D', $D, sid, %i, t>, kdfm(chain(ck0, %i))))>)
   )
   ]

  // loop breaker: [0]
  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) R_Window_Send:
   [ DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0, %i, rh ), !LtkD( $D, skD ), Fr( ~t ) ]
  --[ WinSent( $D, ~sid, rh ), DevStep( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0 ) ]->
   [
   DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0, %i,
            h(<'R0', sign(<'W', <'W', $D, ~sid, ~t>, rh>, skD)>)
   ),
   Out( <<'W', $D, ~sid, ~t>, sign(<'W', <'W', $D, ~sid, ~t>, rh>, skD)> )
   ]

  // loop breaker: [0]
  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) R_Window_Recv:
   [
   SrvSess( $D, $G, sid, ck0, %i, rh ), !PkD( $D, pkD ),
   In( <<'W', $D, sid, t>, s> )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( verify(s, <'W', <'W', $D, sid, t>, rh>, pkD), true ),
  WinAccepted( $D, sid, rh ), SrvStep( $D, $G, sid, ck0 )
  ]->
   [ SrvSess( $D, $G, sid, ck0, %i, h(<'R0', s>) ) ]

  // loop breaker: [0]
  /*

     In( <<'W', $D, sid, t>, s> )
     ]
    --[
    Eq( z, true ), WinAccepted( $D, sid, rh ), SrvStep( $D, $G, sid, ck0 )
    ]->
     [ SrvSess( $D, $G, sid, ck0, %i, h(<'R0', s>) ) ]
    variants (modulo AC)
    1. $D    = $D.21
       pkD   = pkD.24
       rh    = rh.25
       s     = s.26
       sid   = sid.27
       t     = t.28
       z     = verify(s.26, <'W', <'W', $D.21, sid.27, t.28>, rh.25>, pkD.24)
    
    2. $D    = $D.29
       pkD   = pk(x.56)

       sid   = sid.35
       t     = t.36
       z     = true
    // loop breaker: [0]
  */

rule (modulo E) Chain_Forward:
   [ In( chain(x, %i) ) ] --> [ Out( chain(x, (%i%+%1)) ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Reveal_Device_State:
   [ DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0, %i, rh ) ]
  --[
  RevState( ~sid ), RevChain( ck0 ), RevChainAt( ck0, %i ),
  DevRevAt( ck0, %i ), DevStep( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0 )
  ]->
   [ DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0, %i, rh ), Out( chain(ck0, %i) ) ]

  // loop breaker: [0]
  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Reveal_Server_State:
   [ SrvSess( $D, $G, sid, ck0, %i, rh ) ]
  --[
  RevState( sid ), RevChain( ck0 ), RevChainAt( ck0, %i ),
  SrvRevAt( ck0, %i ), SrvStep( $D, $G, sid, ck0 )
  ]->
   [ SrvSess( $D, $G, sid, ck0, %i, rh ), Out( chain(ck0, %i) ) ]

  // loop breaker: [0]
  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

lemma dev_state_origin [reuse, use_induction]:
  all-traces
  "∀ D G sid ck0 #a.
    (DevStep( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #a) ⇒
    (∃ #b. (DevInit( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #a))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ D G sid ck0 #a.
  (DevStep( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #a)
 ∧
  ∀ #b. (DevInit( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #a)"
*/
induction
  case empty_trace
  by contradiction /* from formulas */
next
  case non_empty_trace
  simplify
  solve( (last(#a))  ∥
         (∃ #b. (DevInit( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #b) ∧ (¬(last(#b))) ∧ (#b < #a)) )
    case case_1
    solve( DevStep( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #a )
      case R_Data_Send
      solve( DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0, %i, rh ) ▶₀ #a )
        case R_Data_Send
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case R_Init_Send
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case R_Window_Send
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case Reveal_Device_State
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      qed
    next
      case R_Window_Send
      solve( !LtkD( $D, skD ) ▶₁ #a )
        case Device_Setup
        solve( DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0, %i, rh ) ▶₀ #a )
          case R_Data_Send
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case R_Init_Send
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case R_Window_Send
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case Reveal_Device_State
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        qed
      qed
    next
      case Reveal_Device_State
      solve( DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0, %i, rh ) ▶₀ #a )
        case R_Data_Send
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case R_Init_Send
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case R_Window_Send
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case Reveal_Device_State
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      qed
    qed
  next
    case case_2
    by contradiction /* from formulas */
  qed
qed

lemma chain_key_origin [reuse, use_induction]:
  all-traces
  "∀ x %i #a.
    (!KU( chain(x, %i) ) @ #a) ⇒
    ((∃ #b. (!KU( x ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #a)) ∨
     (∃ #r. (RevChain( x ) @ #r) ∧ (#r < #a)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ x %i #a.
  (!KU( chain(x, %i) ) @ #a)
 ∧
  (∀ #b. (!KU( x ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #a)) ∧
  (∀ #r. (RevChain( x ) @ #r) ⇒ ¬(#r < #a))"
*/
induction
  case empty_trace
  by contradiction /* from formulas */
next
  case non_empty_trace
  simplify
  solve( (last(#a))  ∥
         (∃ #b. (!KU( x ) @ #b) ∧ (¬(last(#b))) ∧ (#b < #a))  ∥
         (∃ #r. (RevChain( x ) @ #r) ∧ (¬(last(#r))) ∧ (#r < #a)) )
    case case_1
    solve( !KU( chain(x, %i) ) @ #a )
      case Chain_Forward
      solve( (∃ #b. (!KU( x ) @ #b) ∧ (¬(last(#b))) ∧ (#b < #vk))  ∥
             (∃ #r. (RevChain( x ) @ #r) ∧ (¬(last(#r))) ∧ (#r < #vk)) )
        case case_1
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case case_2
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      qed
    next
      case Reveal_Device_State
      by contradiction /* from formulas */
    next
      case Reveal_Server_State
      by contradiction /* from formulas */
    next
      case c_chain
      by contradiction /* from formulas */
    qed
  next
    case case_2
    by contradiction /* from formulas */
  next
    case case_3
    by contradiction /* from formulas */
  qed
qed

lemma chain_key_index [reuse, use_induction]:
  all-traces
  "∀ x %i #a.
    (!KU( chain(x, %i) ) @ #a) ⇒
    ((∃ #b. (!KU( x ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #a)) ∨
     (∃ %j #r.
       ((RevChainAt( x, %j ) @ #r) ∧ (#r < #a)) ∧ ((%j = %i) ∨ (%j ⊏ %i))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ x %i #a.
  (!KU( chain(x, %i) ) @ #a)
 ∧
  (∀ #b. (!KU( x ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #a)) ∧
  (∀ %j #r.
    (RevChainAt( x, %j ) @ #r)
   ⇒
    ((¬(#r < #a)) ∨ ((¬(%j = %i)) ∧ (¬(%j ⊏ %i)))))"
*/
induction
  case empty_trace
  by contradiction /* from formulas */
next
  case non_empty_trace
  simplify
  solve( (last(#a))  ∥
         (∃ #b. (!KU( x ) @ #b) ∧ (¬(last(#b))) ∧ (#b < #a))  ∥
         (∃ %j #r.
           (RevChainAt( x, %j ) @ #r)
          ∧
           (¬(last(#r))) ∧ (#r < #a) ∧ (((%j = %i) ∨ (%j ⊏ %i)))) )
    case case_1
    solve( (∃ #b. (!KU( x ) @ #b) ∧ #b < #a)  ∥
           (∃ #r. (RevChain( x ) @ #r) ∧ #r < #a) )
      case case_1
      by contradiction /* from formulas */
    next
      case case_2
      solve( RevChain( x ) @ #r )
        case Reveal_Device_State
        simplify
        solve( !LtkD( $D, skD ) ▶₀ #b )
          case Device_Setup
          solve( !PkG( $G, pkG ) ▶₁ #b )
            case Gateway_Setup
            solve( !KU( chain(hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                   <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                              %i.1)
                   ) @ #a )
              case Chain_Forward
              simplify
              solve( (∃ #b.
                       (!KU( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                        ) @ #b)
                      ∧
                       #b < #vk)  ∥
                     (∃ #r.
                       (RevChain( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                        ) @ #r)
                      ∧
                       #r < #vk) )
                case case_1
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              next
                case case_2
                solve( (∃ #b.
                         (!KU( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                          ) @ #b)
                        ∧
                         (¬(last(#b))) ∧ (#b < #vk))  ∥
                       (∃ %j #r.
                         (RevChainAt( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                           <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                                      %j
                          ) @ #r)
                        ∧
                         (¬(last(#r))) ∧ (#r < #vk) ∧ (((%j = %i) ∨ (%j ⊏ %i)))) )
                  case case_1
                  by contradiction /* from formulas */
                next
                  case case_2
                  by contradiction /* contradictory subterm store */
                qed
              qed
            next
              case Reveal_Device_State
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case Reveal_Server_State
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case c_chain
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            qed
          qed
        qed
      next
        case Reveal_Server_State
        simplify
        solve( !KU( chain(x, %i.1) ) @ #a )
          case Chain_Forward
          simplify
          solve( (∃ #b. (!KU( x ) @ #b) ∧ #b < #vk)  ∥
                 (∃ #r. (RevChain( x ) @ #r) ∧ #r < #vk) )
            case case_1
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          next
            case case_2
            solve( (∃ #b. (!KU( x ) @ #b) ∧ (¬(last(#b))) ∧ (#b < #vk))  ∥
                   (∃ %j #r.
                     (RevChainAt( x, %j ) @ #r)
                    ∧
                     (¬(last(#r))) ∧ (#r < #vk) ∧ (((%j = %i) ∨ (%j ⊏ %i)))) )
              case case_1
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case case_2
              by contradiction /* contradictory subterm store */
            qed
          qed
        next
          case Reveal_Device_State
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case Reveal_Server_State
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case c_chain
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        qed
      qed
    qed
  next
    case case_2
    by contradiction /* from formulas */
  next
    case case_3
    by contradiction /* from formulas */
  qed
qed

lemma srv_state_origin [reuse, use_induction]:
  all-traces
  "∀ D G sid ck0 #a.
    (SrvStep( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #a) ⇒
    (∃ #b. (SrvInit( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #a))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ D G sid ck0 #a.
  (SrvStep( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #a)
 ∧
  ∀ #b. (SrvInit( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #a)"
*/
induction
  case empty_trace
  by contradiction /* from formulas */
next
  case non_empty_trace
  simplify
  solve( (last(#a))  ∥
         (∃ #b. (SrvInit( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #b) ∧ (¬(last(#b))) ∧ (#b < #a)) )
    case case_1
    solve( SrvStep( D, G, sid, ck0 ) @ #a )
      case R_Data_Recv
      solve( SrvSess( $D, $G, sid, ck0, %i, rh ) ▶₀ #a )
        case R_Data_Recv
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case R_Init_Recv
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case R_Window_Recv
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case Reveal_Server_State
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      qed
    next
      case R_Window_Recv
      solve( !PkD( $D, pk(x) ) ▶₁ #a )
        case Device_Setup
        solve( !KU( sign(<'W', <'W', $D, sid, t>, rh>, ~skD) ) @ #vk.8 )
          case R_Window_Send
          solve( !LtkD( $D, skD.1 ) ▶₀ #b )
            case Device_Setup
            solve( !PkG( $G.1, pkG ) ▶₁ #b )
              case Gateway_Setup
              solve( SrvSess( $D, $G, ~sid, ck0, %i, rh ) ▶₀ #a )
                case R_Data_Recv
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              next
                case R_Init_Recv
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              next
                case R_Window_Recv
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              next
                case Reveal_Server_State
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              qed
            qed
          qed
        next
          case c_sign
          solve( !KU( ~skD ) @ #vk.11 )
            case Reveal_Device
            solve( SrvSess( $D, $G, sid, ck0, %i, rh ) ▶₀ #a )
              case R_Data_Recv
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case R_Init_Recv
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case R_Window_Recv
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case Reveal_Server_State
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    next
      case Reveal_Server_State
      solve( SrvSess( $D, $G, sid, ck0, %i, rh ) ▶₀ #a )
        case R_Data_Recv
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case R_Init_Recv
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case R_Window_Recv
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      next
        case Reveal_Server_State
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      qed
    qed
  next
    case case_2
    by contradiction /* from formulas */
  qed
qed

lemma routine_executable [heuristic={states_last}]:
  exists-trace
  "∃ D G sid m %i #a.
    (((RRecv( D, G, sid, m, %i ) @ #a) ∧ (¬(∃ X #r. RevG( X ) @ #r))) ∧
     (¬(∃ X #r. RevD( X ) @ #r))) ∧
    (¬(∃ X #r. RevState( X ) @ #r))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ D G sid m %i #a.
  (RRecv( D, G, sid, m, %i ) @ #a)
 ∧
  (∀ X #r. (RevG( X ) @ #r) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ X #r. (RevD( X ) @ #r) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ X #r. (RevState( X ) @ #r) ⇒ ⊥)"
*/
simplify
solve( !KU( sign(<'I', 'I', $D, $G, sid, E, n, t>, x) ) @ #vk.23 )
  case R_Init_Send
  solve( !KU( ~sid ) @ #vk.9 )
    case R_Data_Send_case_1
    solve( !KU( ~n ) @ #vk.22 )
      case R_Init_Send
      solve( !KU( ~t ) @ #vk.23 )
        case R_Init_Send
        solve( !LtkG( $G, ~skG ) ▶₀ #b )
          case Gateway_Setup
          solve( !PkD( $D, pk(~skD) ) ▶₁ #b )
            case Device_Setup
            solve( !KU( senc(<m, 'D', $D, ~sid, %i, t.1>,
                             kdfm(chain(hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                                        %i)))
                   ) @ #vk.20 )
              case R_Data_Send
              solve( !KU( ~t.1 ) @ #vk.20 )
                case R_Data_Send_case_1
                solve( !KU( 'g'^~e ) @ #vk.23 )
                  case R_Init_Send
                  solve( SrvSess( $D, $G, ~sid,
                                  hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>), %i, rh
                         ) ▶₀ #a )
                    case R_Init_Recv
                    solve( DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid,
                                    hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>), %i, rh
                           ) ▶₀ #vr.4 )
                      case R_Data_Send
                      solve( DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid,
                                      hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>), %1, rh.1
                             ) ▶₀ #vr.17 )
                        case R_Init_Send
                        solve( DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid,
                                        hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>), %i, rh
                               ) ▶₀ #vr.25 )
                          case R_Data_Send
                          solve( DevSess( $D, $G, ~sid,
                                          hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>), %i, rh
                                 ) ▶₀ #vr.26 )
                            case R_Init_Send
                            SOLVED // trace found
                          qed
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma routine_secrecy [heuristic={states_last}]:
  all-traces
  "∀ D G sid m %i #a.
    (RSent( D, G, sid, m, %i ) @ #a) ⇒
    (((¬(∃ #k. K( m ) @ #k)) ∨ (∃ #r. RevG( G ) @ #r)) ∨
     (∃ #r. RevState( sid ) @ #r))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ D G sid m %i #a.
  (RSent( D, G, sid, m, %i ) @ #a)
 ∧
  (∃ #k. (K( m ) @ #k)) ∧
  (∀ #r. (RevG( G ) @ #r) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ #r. (RevState( sid ) @ #r) ⇒ ⊥)"
*/
simplify
solve( !LtkD( $D, skD ) ▶₀ #b )
  case Device_Setup
  solve( !KU( ~m ) @ #vk )
    case R_Data_Send
    solve( !PkG( $G, pkG ) ▶₁ #b )
      case Gateway_Setup
      solve( !KU( kdfm(chain(hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                  <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                             %i))
             ) @ #vk.1 )
        case c_kdfm
        solve( (∃ #b.
                 (!KU( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                  ) @ #b)
                ∧
                 #b < #vk.2)  ∥
               (∃ %j #r.
                 (RevChainAt( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                   <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                              %j
                  ) @ #r)
                ∧
                 (#r < #vk.2) ∧ (((%j = %i) ∨ (%j ⊏ %i)))) )
          case case_1
          solve( (∃ #b.
                   (!KU( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                    ) @ #b)
                  ∧
                   #b < #vk.2)  ∥
                 (∃ #r.
                   (RevChain( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                    ) @ #r)
                  ∧
                   #r < #vk.2) )
            case case_1
            solve( !KU( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                   ) @ #b.1 )
              case c_hkdf
              solve( !KU( ~n ) @ #vk.4 )
                case R_Init_Send
                solve( !KU( ~sid ) @ #vk.14 )
                  case R_Data_Send_case_1
                  solve( !KU( ~t ) @ #vk.16 )
                    case R_Init_Send
                    solve( !KU( 'g'^(~e*~skG) ) @ #vk.10 )
                      case Gateway_Setup
                      by solve( !KU( ~e ) @ #vk.17 )
                    next
                      case R_Init_Send
                      solve( !KU( ~skG ) @ #vk.17 )
                        case Reveal_Gateway
                        by contradiction /* from formulas */
                      qed
                    next
                      case c_exp
                      by solve( !KU( ~e ) @ #vk.19 )
                    qed
                  qed
                next
                  case R_Data_Send_case_2
                  solve( !KU( ~t ) @ #vk.16 )
                    case R_Init_Send
                    solve( !KU( kdfm(chain(hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                                <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                                           %i.1))
                           ) @ #vk.17 )
                      case c_kdfm
                      solve( (∃ #b.
                               (!KU( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                                ) @ #b)
                              ∧
                               #b < #vk.18)  ∥
                             (∃ %j #r.
                               (RevChainAt( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                                 <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                                            %j
                                ) @ #r)
                              ∧
                               (#r < #vk.18) ∧ (((%j = %i.1) ∨ (%j ⊏ %i.1)))) )
                        case case_1
                        by contradiction /* cyclic */
                      next
                        case case_2
                        solve( (∃ #b.
                                 (!KU( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                                  ) @ #b)
                                ∧
                                 #b < #vk.18)  ∥
                               (∃ #r.
                                 (RevChain( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                                  ) @ #r)
                                ∧
                                 #r < #vk.18) )
                          case case_1
                          by contradiction /* cyclic */
                        next
                          case case_2
                          solve( (%j = %i.1)  ∥ (%j ⊏ %i.1) )
                            case case_1
                            solve( RevChainAt( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                                    <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                                               %j
                                   ) @ #r )
                              case Reveal_Device_State
                              by contradiction /* from formulas */
                            next
                              case Reveal_Server_State
                              by contradiction /* cyclic */
                            qed
                          next
                            case case_2
                            solve( RevChainAt( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                                    <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                                               %j
                                   ) @ #r )
                              case Reveal_Device_State
                              by contradiction /* from formulas */
                            next
                              case Reveal_Server_State
                              by contradiction /* cyclic */
                            qed
                          qed
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                next
                  case R_Init_Send
                  solve( !KU( ~t ) @ #vk.16 )
                    case R_Init_Send
                    solve( !KU( 'g'^(~e*~skG) ) @ #vk.10 )
                      case Gateway_Setup
                      by solve( !KU( ~e ) @ #vk.17 )
                    next
                      case R_Init_Send
                      solve( !KU( ~skG ) @ #vk.17 )
                        case Reveal_Gateway
                        by contradiction /* from formulas */

                    next
                      case c_exp
                      by solve( !KU( ~e ) @ #vk.19 )

                  qed

                    solve( !KU( 'g'^(~e*~skG) ) @ #vk.10 )
                      case Gateway_Setup
                      by solve( !KU( ~e ) @ #vk.17 )
                    next
                      case R_Init_Send
                      solve( !KU( ~skG ) @ #vk.17 )
                        case Reveal_Gateway
                        by contradiction /* from formulas */
                      qed
                    next
                      case c_exp
                      by solve( !KU( ~e ) @ #vk.19 )
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          next
            case case_2
            solve( RevChain( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                  <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                   ) @ #r )
              case Reveal_Device_State

              case Reveal_Server_State
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            qed
          qed
        next
          case case_2
          solve( (∃ #b.
                   (!KU( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                    ) @ #b)
                  ∧
                   #b < #vk.2)  ∥
                 (∃ #r.
                   (RevChain( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>)
                    ) @ #r)
                  ∧
                   #r < #vk.2) )
            case case_1
            solve( (%j = %i)  ∥ (%j ⊏ %i) )
              case case_1
              solve( RevChainAt( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                      <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                                 %i
                     ) @ #r )

                by contradiction /* from formulas */

                by contradiction /* from formulas */

              solve( RevChainAt( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                      <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                                 %j
                     ) @ #r )
                case Reveal_Device_State
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              next
                case Reveal_Server_State
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              qed
            qed
          next
            case case_2
            solve( (%j = %i)  ∥ (%j ⊏ %i) )
              case case_1
              solve( RevChainAt( hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n,
                                      <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),

lemma routine_authentication [heuristic={states_last}]:
  all-traces
  "∀ D G sid m %i #a.
    (RRecv( D, G, sid, m, %i ) @ #a) ⇒
    ((((∃ #b. (RSent( D, G, sid, m, %i ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #a)) ∨
       (∃ #r. (RevD( D ) @ #r) ∧ (#r < #a))) ∨
      (∃ #r. RevG( G ) @ #r)) ∨
     (∃ #r. RevState( sid ) @ #r))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ D G sid m %i #a.
  (RRecv( D, G, sid, m, %i ) @ #a)
 ∧
  (∀ #b. (RSent( D, G, sid, m, %i ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #a)) ∧
  (∀ #r. (RevD( D ) @ #r) ⇒ ¬(#r < #a)) ∧
  (∀ #r. (RevG( G ) @ #r) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ #r. (RevState( sid ) @ #r) ⇒ ⊥)"
*/
simplify
solve( !KU( sign(<'I', 'I', $D, $G, sid, E, n, t>, x) ) @ #vk.23 )
  case R_Init_Send
  solve( !KU( ~sid ) @ #vk.9 )
    case R_Data_Send_case_1
    solve( !KU( ~n ) @ #vk.22 )
      case R_Init_Send
      solve( !KU( ~t ) @ #vk.23 )
        case R_Init_Send
        solve( !LtkG( $G, ~skG ) ▶₀ #b )
          case Gateway_Setup
          solve( !PkD( $D, pk(~skD) ) ▶₁ #b )
            case Device_Setup
            solve( !KU( senc(<m, 'D', $D, ~sid, %i, t.1>,
                             kdfm(chain(hkdf('g'^(~e*~skG), ~n, <'I', $D, $G, ~sid, 'g'^~e, ~n, ~t>),
                                        %i)))
                   ) @ #vk.20 )
              case R_Data_Send
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
